# Near misses

C that compiles close to a function but not to its bytes: a starting point, never linked
and never counted. Move a file into `src/` once `tools/build.py` places it.

Recorded candidate sizes, profiles and differences describe the retained experiments, not
verified behavior. Declaration repairs and symbol renaming are not new match measurements.
Drafts marked G8 need a `-g8.c` source unit when checked again.

Extracted drafts may still need the original source unit's types and declarations. Their
recorded checks describe the original compilation unit, not standalone syntax or verified behavior.

| Function | Size | Bytes that differ |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| [`func_00282C90`](game/func_00282C90.c) | 8 | 2 instructions differ; retail stores the double with the FPU's `sdc1 $f0,0($a0)`, this cc1 moves it through a GPR pair (`sd $a1,0($a0)`) - the PS2 double-in-`$f0/$f1` argument convention is not what this cc1 emits |
| [`func_002828B8`](game/func_002828B8.c) | 16 | Candidate is 12 bytes; the gp-relative float constant and the multiplication are right, but this cc1 moves the `jr $ra` into the delay slot of the `lwc1` where retail leaves a nop.  Same wall for func_002828F0, func_00282928 and func_00282938 |
| [`func_00283CB8`](game/func_00283CB8.c) | 16 | Candidate is 32 bytes; retail keeps the truncated integer in the FPU (`cvt.w.s $f0,$f12` ; `cvt.s.w $f0,$f0`), this cc1 moves it through a GPR (`mfc1`/`mtc1`) before `cvt.s.w` |
| [`func_00283CF0`](game/func_00283CF0.c) | 16 | 2 instructions differ; retail converts in place (`cvt.w.s $f12,$f12` ; `mfc1 $v0,$f12`), this cc1 always converts into a fresh FPR (`cvt.w.s $f0,$f12` ; `mfc1 $v0,$f0`) |
| [`func_0029CF58`](game/func_0029CF58.c) | 32 | Candidate is 36 bytes and two instructions differ; retail builds the SPR block address in `$at` (`lui $at,0x1000` ; `ori $at,$at,0xD400`) and keeps the constant 0x100 as `ori $v0,$zero,0x100` until the last store, which sits in the `jr $ra` delay slot; this cc1 builds the address in `$v0`, materialises 0x100 as `addiu` before it, and leaves a nop in the delay slot |
| [`func_0029DCD0`](game/func_0029DCD0.c) | 44 | Candidate is 40 bytes and the branch shape differs; retail keeps the redundant 64-bit zeroings (`daddu $v0,$zero,$zero`) of the result in the delay slots of both branches, so the result is 64-bit, while this cc1 merges the two tests into one (`bgezl` + `movz`) |
| [`func_0029DC90`](game/func_0029DC90.c) | 60 | Candidate is 64 bytes and the branch shape differs; same wall as func_0029DCD0 - the 64-bit result and the three nested tests are merged into one branch plus `movz`/`movn` by this cc1 |
| [`func_0028CC98`](game/func_0028CC98.c) | 72 | 6 of 18 words differ: the stores and their order are right (the two halfword stores have to be written in the reverse order in the source), but the scheduler materialises the constants in another order (retail 8, 0xD2, -2, 0xC8, 0x1E, this cc1 8, 0xC8, 0xD2, 0x1E, -2) and puts the store of the gp address before the store of the 8. The body needs the -G8 profile (two $gp-relative objects), so it belongs in 0028BDB0-g8.c |
| [`func_002A90C8`](game/func_002A90C8.c) | 36 | Candidate is 32 bytes: the call shape is right (the fifth argument travels in $t0, the ABI's fifth argument register) but this cc1 splits the symbol's address, the %hi before the `sd $ra` and the %lo in the call's delay slot; retail keeps lui+addiu together and leaves the call's delay slot a nop. With -mno-split-addresses the halves stay together but the `ld $ra` then sinks into the call's delay slot, so the flag alone does not give retail's nine words |
| [`func_00270280`](game/func_00270280.c) | 52 | 60-byte candidate (wave 10): the resident-table walk (entries 0xA0 apart, id word at +0x86, table 0x1E00 long); retail branches likely ("bnel") on the id test and keeps the increment in the annulled delay slot, this cc1 emits a plain "beq" with the not-found path as a separate block.  Measured: the bound test is a signed "slt" in retail - an explicit `(int)` cast on the pointers produces it, a plain pointer comparison gives "sltu", and `signed char *` does not change it |
| [`func_002A04B8`](game/func_002A04B8.c) | 48 | 44-byte candidate (wave 10): the reader of the three bytes func_002A0488 packs - retail loads the 64-bit word into $a0 (the parameter register it has just read), shifts it three times and masks the three results before storing them; this cc1 keeps the word in $v0, reuses $a0 for the second result and interleaves the shifts with the stores (volatile destinations change nothing) |
| [`func_002B68C8`](game/func_002B68C8.c) | 108 | 112-byte candidate (wave 10): the call-state initialiser at 0x001A63A8; the candidate has retail's exact size and every instruction but one - retail keeps the final store (the -1 at +0x24) in the delay slot of the call to func_002B66F0, this cc1 schedules it before the last flag store; making every store volatile keeps the retail order exactly but the delay slot then holds a nop (a volatile store is not moved into a delay slot) |
| [`func_00349610`](game/func_00349610.c) | 24 | Retail keeps the plain pointer in `$v0` for the first store and a 64-bit copy of it in the pair `($a0,$a1)` for the second one, so the stores cannot be reordered and the second store's 12 is folded into its displacement; this cc1 folds the 64-bit copy away (`dsll`/`dsra` into one register) and then reorders the two stores. Every 64-bit spelling tried (`long`/`long long` locals, `+ 0`, `+ 0x100000000L`, index and pointer casts, the SDK compiler and `-O1`/`-O3`) either drops the `daddu` or changes the store order |
| [`func_0028AB20`](game/func_0028AB20.c) | 76 | The u16 scan, its 8-byte stride and the counted return are recovered; retail keeps the table pointer in a 64-bit register (the `daddu $v1, $a1, $zero` copy, the stride added to its low word only), this cc1 keeps a 32-bit pointer and sign-extends it (dsll/dsra) at every use |
| [`func_0028BD68`](game/func_0028BD68.c) | 72 | The loop, the 0x1C stride and the seven-item count are recovered; what is left is allocation - retail keeps the four values in $a0-$a3, this cc1 in another order of $a0-$a3 (or in $s0-$s3 without arguments) and adds a load-delay nop after the value load |
| [`func_002939D0`](game/func_002939D0.c) | 128 | The whole body is recovered (the 0x1057-granular arena address, the store in D_001A7288, the call to func_002B7C10); three words are left - retail keeps the final `& 0xFFFFFFF0` (this cc1 folds it, the value only reaches an address), retail materialises 0x1FF7FF0 as `lui`+`addiu` and this cc1 as `lui`+`ori`, and retail re-materialises D_001A7288's address for the reload where this cc1 keeps it in a register |
| [`func_0028A278`](game/func_0028A278.c) | 128 | Every instruction and every address is right; the residue is how the globals' addresses are materialised - retail puts each one-off address in `$at` and offsets from it (`lui $at,0x1B; sw $v0,0x194C($at)`), this cc1 keeps them in ordinary registers and offsets by zero (the same word count, one different word per global) |

The two 16-byte copies differ: the retail materialises the pointer (`addiu $a0,$s0,0x10` / `sq $v0,0x0($a0)`), this compiler folds the constant offset into the `sq`. The fold happens in the CSE pass (`find_best_addr` in gcc/cse.c): the address is `(mem:TI (plus:SI (reg) (const_int off)) 0)` and `mips_address_cost` gives register+small-constant the cost 1, the same as a plain register, so the tie goes to the plus. The TImode displacement range is [-32768, 32752]; an offset outside it makes the reload materialise the address - the retail's shape with the wrong immediate. The float constants are hoisted in front of the copy, and the second call's delay slot holds the store to `arg0+0x98` where the retail has `addiu $a1,$s0,0xF0`. |
